*    Each bolt extractor set is made of high-hardness chrome-molybdenum steel for superior durability and longer life

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the bolt extractor kit effective, taking less than a minute to remove nuts and bolts, and one customer reports success with a rounded oil drain bolt. They appreciate its quality and consider it essential for DIY mechanics, with one noting it saved them $1500 in shop charges. The durability and grip features receive mixed feedback - while some say it handles stress well and grips well, others report pieces breaking off and slipping during use.

# Product Summary: 35 Pcs Bolt Extractor Kit, 1/4" & 3/8" Drive Impact Bolt & Nut Remover Set | for Damaged, Rusted, Rounded-Off, Stripped Fasteners, 6-19mm, Cr-Mo Steel, Reverse Spiral Flute, Storage Case

From THINKWORK

4.6 out of 5 stars, 1,893 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0D9S4DT1T?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)
50+ bought in past week

## Price

One-time purchase: $46.97

[Purchasing options](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0D9S4DT1T?ref=dp_product_quick_view#buybox)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0D9S4DT1T?ref=dp_product_quick_view#dynamic-aod-ingress-box)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0D9S4DT1T?ref=dp_product_quick_view#unqualifiedBuyBox)

## About this Item

*   Each bolt extractor set is made of high-hardness chrome-molybdenum steel for superior durability and longer life
*   The internal thread with reverse spiral flutes is designed to bite down for maximum gripping power. The more you turn, the tighter the bolt extractor socket set grips
*   The standard 1/4 & 3/8 inch square drive and socket adapter make the nut extractor socket set compatible with hand, air, or power tools—the knockout pin for easier removal
*   The heavy-duty plastic blow-molded carrying case keeps the lug nut extractor organized, with easy storage and carrying
*   Clear laser-etched markings for easy identification even in low light conditions

## Product Description

 Bolt Extractor Kit, 35 PCS Nut Extractor Socket Set, Easy Out Impact Bolt Remover Set, Lug Nut Removal Tool for Damaged, Broken, Rusted, Rounded-Off Bolts, Nuts, Screws
